data-agent-docs-index="true"><h2>Documentation Index</h2><p>Fetch the complete documentation index at: <a href="https://docs.whop.com/llms.txt">https://docs.whop.com/llms.txt</a></p><p>Use this file to discover all available pages before exploring further.</p></blockquote><span data-as="p">Two things can happen after a payment goes through. <strong>Refunds</strong> are a reversal you (or your You don’t always control how a dispute lands, but you can respond with evidence.</span>

<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="refunds"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#refunds" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="gray" height="12px" viewBox="0 0 576 512"><path d="M0 256C0 167.6 71.6 96 160 96h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C98.1 144 48 194.1 48 256s50.1 112 112 112h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C71.6 416 0 344.4 0 256zm576 0c0 88.4-71.6 160-160 160H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c61.9 0 112-50.1 112-112s-50.1-112-112-112H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c88.4 0 160 71.6 160 160zM184 232H392c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H184c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Refunds</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Refunds reverse a successful payment. Whop processes them through the original payment processor. The matching membership status updates automatically.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="issue-a-refund"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#issue-a-refund" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="gray" height="12px" viewBox="0 0 576 512"><path d="M0 256C0 167.6 71.6 96 160 96h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C98.1 144 48 194.1 48 256s50.1 112 112 112h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C71.6 416 0 344.4 0 256zm576 0c0 88.4-71.6 160-160 160H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c61.9 0 112-50.1 112-112s-50.1-112-112-112H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c88.4 0 160 71.6 160 160zM184 232H392c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H184c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Issue a refund</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Refunds are created against a payment, not directly on the refunds resource. Call <code>payments.refund(id, ...)</code>. Omit <code>partial_amount</code> for a full refund, or pass it for a partial.</span>

<span data-as="p">The response is the updated <code>Payment</code> object. The <code>refunded_amount</code> field reflects the cumulative refunded total. You can call <code>refund</code> multiple times against a payment up to its full amount.</span>

Add it from the <a class="link" href="/developer/guides/permissions">Permissions guide</a> before publishing your app.</span></div></div>

<span data-as="p">Refunds have their own resource if you need to look them up after the fact.</span>

<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="disputes"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#disputes" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="gray" height="12px" viewBox="0 0 576 512"><path d="M0 256C0 167.6 71.6 96 160 96h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C98.1 144 48 194.1 48 256s50.1 112 112 112h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C71.6 416 0 344.4 0 256zm576 0c0 88.4-71.6 160-160 160H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c61.9 0 112-50.1 112-112s-50.1-112-112-112H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c88.4 0 160 71.6 160 160zM184 232H392c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H184c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Disputes</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">A dispute (chargeback) opens when the buyer’s bank challenges a payment. You have a window to respond with evidence before the bank decides. Whop forwards the dispute to you, you submit evidence, the bank rules, and the dispute closes.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="watch-for-incoming-disputes"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#watch-for-incoming-disputes" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="gray" height="12px" viewBox="0 0 576 512"><path d="M0 256C0 167.6 71.6 96 160 96h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C98.1 144 48 194.1 48 256s50.1 112 112 112h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C71.6 416 0 344.4 0 256zm576 0c0 88.4-71.6 160-160 160H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c61.9 0 112-50.1 112-112s-50.1-112-112-112H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c88.4 0 160 71.6 160 160zM184 232H392c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H184c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Watch for incoming disputes</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Two events to subscribe to:</span>
<div data-table-wrapper="true" class="overflow-x-auto flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-[var(--page-padding)] max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div class="px-[var(--page-padding)] grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px] [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums"><thead><tr><th>Event</th><th>Meaning</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><a class="link" href="/api-reference/dispute-alerts/disputealert-created"><code>dispute_alert.created</code></a></td><td>Early warning before a formal dispute. Some processors give a heads-up. Not every alert turns into a dispute.</td></tr><tr><td><a class="link" href="/api-reference/disputes/dispute-created"><code>dispute.created</code></a></td><td>Formal dispute opened. Response window starts.</td></tr><tr><td><a class="link" href="/api-reference/disputes/dispute-updated"><code>dispute.updated</code></a></td><td>Status changes (e.g. from <code>needs_response</code> to <code>under_review</code>, then <code>won</code> or <code>lost</code>).</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="list-and-retrieve"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#list-and-retrieve" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="gray" height="12px" viewBox="0 0 576 512"><path d="M0 256C0 167.6 71.6 96 160 96h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C98.1 144 48 194.1 48 256s50.1 112 112 112h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C71.6 416 0 344.4 0 256zm576 0c0 88.4-71.6 160-160 160H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c61.9 0 112-50.1 112-112s-50.1-112-112-112H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c88.4 0 160 71.6 160 160zM184 232H392c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H184c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">List and retrieve</span></h3>

style="background-color:#ffffff;--shiki-dark-bg:#0B0C0E;color:#1f2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4" language="typescript"><code language="typescript" numberOfLines="7"><span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">for</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0"> await</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> (</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">const</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#4FC1FF"> page</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#569CD6"> of</span><span <span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  company_id:</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> &quot;biz_xxxxxxxxxxxxx&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">})) {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  console</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">.</span><span style="color:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA">log</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">page</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">);</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">}</span></span>
<span class="line"></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#569CD6">const</span><span style="color:#0550AE;--shiki-dark:#4FC1FF"> dispute</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4"> =</span><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0"> await</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE"> client</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">.</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">disputes</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">.</span><span style="color:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA">retrieve</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;dp_xxxxxxxxxxxxx&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">);</span></span>
</code></pre></div></div><div class="print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div><div data-state="inactive" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_ab5dktbsnlht5dabsnpfdb_-trigger-1" hidden="" id="radix-_R_ab5dktbsnlht5dabsnpfdb_-content-1" tabindex="-1" class="w-full min-w-full max-w-full h-full max-h-full relative [&amp;_[data-component-part=code-block-root]]:!rounded-xt"></div><div data-state="inactive" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_ab5dktbsnlht5dabsnpfdb_-trigger-2" hidden="" id="radix-_R_ab5dktbsnlht5dabsnpfdb_-content-2" tabindex="-1" class="w-full min-w-full max-w-full h-full max-h-full relative [&amp;_[data-component-part=code-block-root]]:!rounded-xt"></div></div></div>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="respond-with-evidence"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#respond-with-evidence" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="gray" height="12px" viewBox="0 0 576 512"><path d="M0 256C0 167.6 71.6 96 160 96h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C98.1 144 48 194.1 48 256s50.1 112 112 112h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C71.6 416 0 344.4 0 256zm576 0c0 88.4-71.6 160-160 160H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c61.9 0 112-50.1 112-112s-50.1-112-112-112H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c88.4 0 160 71.6 160 160zM184 232H392c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H184c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Respond with evidence</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Disputes have a two-step response: stage your evidence with <code>updateEvidence</code>, then submit with <code>submitEvidence</code>. Update is idempotent. You can edit fields up until you submit.</span>

style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955">// Stage evidence (call as many times as needed)</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#CF222E;--shiki-dark:#C586C0">await</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE"> client</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">.</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">disputes</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">.</span><span style="color:#8250DF;--shiki-dark:#DCDCAA">updateEvidence</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">(</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178">&quot;dp_xxxxxxxxxxxxx&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">, {</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  customer_name:</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> &quot;Jane Doe&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  customer_email_address:</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> &quot;jane@example.com&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>
<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  product_description:</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> &quot;Annual subscription to Acme Pro, purchased 2026-01-15.&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#9CDCFE">  refund_policy_disclosure:</span><span style="color:#0A3069;--shiki-dark:#CE9178"> &quot;Customer agreed to no-refunds policy at checkout.&quot;</span><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">,</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#1F2328;--shiki-dark:#D4D4D4">});</span></span>

<span class="line"><span style="color:#6E7781;--shiki-dark:#6A9955">// Submit evidence to the processor. After this, you can no longer edit.</span></span>

</code></pre></div></div><div class="print:hidden" data-fade-overlay="true" aria-hidden="true" style="--fade-color-light:#ffffff;--fade-color-dark:#0B0C0E"></div></div><div data-state="inactive" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_an5dktbsnlht5dabsnpfdb_-trigger-1" hidden="" id="radix-_R_an5dktbsnlht5dabsnpfdb_-content-1" tabindex="-1" class="w-full min-w-full max-w-full h-full max-h-full relative [&amp;_[data-component-part=code-block-root]]:!rounded-xt"></div><div data-state="inactive" data-orientation="horizontal" role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby="radix-_R_an5dktbsnlht5dabsnpfdb_-trigger-2" hidden="" id="radix-_R_an5dktbsnlht5dabsnpfdb_-content-2" tabindex="-1" class="w-full min-w-full max-w-full h-full max-h-full relative [&amp;_[data-component-part=code-block-root]]:!rounded-xt"></div></div></div>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="evidence-fields"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#evidence-fields" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="gray" height="12px" viewBox="0 0 576 512"><path d="M0 256C0 167.6 71.6 96 160 96h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C98.1 144 48 194.1 48 256s50.1 112 112 112h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C71.6 416 0 344.4 0 256zm576 0c0 88.4-71.6 160-160 160H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c61.9 0 112-50.1 112-112s-50.1-112-112-112H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c88.4 0 160 71.6 160 160zM184 232H392c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H184c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Evidence fields</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Every field is optional, but the more you provide, the stronger the response. Common ones:</span>
<div data-table-wrapper="true" class="overflow-x-auto flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-[var(--page-padding)] max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div class="px-[var(--page-padding)] grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px] [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums"><thead><tr><th>Field</th><th>What to put in it</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>customer_name</code> / <code>customer_email_address</code></td><td>Identifying info you have on file</td></tr><tr><td><code>product_description</code></td><td>What the customer bought, in plain language</td></tr><tr><td><code>service_date</code></td><td>When the service was rendered (ISO 8601)</td></tr><tr><td><code>billing_address</code></td><td>The address used at checkout</td></tr><tr><td><code>customer_communication_attachment</code></td><td>Email or chat threads showing the customer engaged</td></tr><tr><td><code>cancellation_policy_attachment</code> / <code>cancellation_policy_disclosure</code></td><td>Your policy and proof the customer accepted it</td></tr><tr><td><code>refund_policy_attachment</code> / <code>refund_policy_disclosure</code></td><td>Same for refunds</td></tr><tr><td><code>refund_refusal_explanation</code></td><td>Why you didn’t refund (e.g. usage past return window)</td></tr><tr><td><code>access_activity_log</code></td><td>Login or usage logs proving the customer used the product</td></tr><tr><td><code>uncategorized_attachment</code></td><td>Anything else relevant</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div>
<span data-as="p">Attachment fields take an uploaded file ID. Upload first via the <a class="link" href="/developer/guides/upload-files">Files API</a>, then pass <code>file.id</code>.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="dispute-statuses"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#dispute-statuses" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="gray" height="12px" viewBox="0 0 576 512"><path d="M0 256C0 167.6 71.6 96 160 96h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C98.1 144 48 194.1 48 256s50.1 112 112 112h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C71.6 416 0 344.4 0 256zm576 0c0 88.4-71.6 160-160 160H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c61.9 0 112-50.1 112-112s-50.1-112-112-112H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c88.4 0 160 71.6 160 160zM184 232H392c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H184c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Dispute statuses</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">The dispute moves through a state machine. There are two phases: the early-warning phase (statuses prefixed <code>warning_</code>, surfaced via <code>dispute_alert.created</code>) and the formal dispute phase. Every status the SDK can return:</span>
<div data-table-wrapper="true" class="overflow-x-auto flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-[var(--page-padding)] max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div class="px-[var(--page-padding)] grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px] [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums"><thead><tr><th>Status</th><th>Phase</th><th>Meaning</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td><code>warning_needs_response</code></td><td>Alert</td><td>A dispute alert is open. You can preempt with a refund or response before it escalates.</td></tr><tr><td><code>warning_under_review</code></td><td>Alert</td><td>You responded to the alert; the processor is deciding whether to escalate.</td></tr><tr><td><code>warning_closed</code></td><td>Alert</td><td>The alert resolved without becoming a formal dispute.</td></tr><tr><td><code>needs_response</code></td><td>Dispute</td><td>Formal dispute open. Submit evidence before the response window closes.</td></tr><tr><td><code>under_review</code></td><td>Dispute</td><td>You submitted evidence; the processor is deciding.</td></tr><tr><td><code>won</code></td><td>Dispute</td><td>Funds stay with you.</td></tr><tr><td><code>lost</code></td><td>Dispute</td><td>Funds go back to the buyer. The original payment is reversed.</td></tr><tr><td><code>closed</code></td><td>Dispute</td><td>Dispute closed without a formal won/lost outcome (e.g. withdrawn).</td></tr><tr><td><code>other</code></td><td>Either</td><td>Catch-all for statuses that don’t fit the above. Treat as informational.</td></tr></tbody></table></div></div>
<div class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 dark:border-blue-900 dark:bg-blue-600/20" data-callout-type="note"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 14 14" fill="currentColor" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" class="size-4 text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300" aria-label="Note"><path fill-rule="evenodd" clip-rule="evenodd" d="M7 1.3C10.14 1.3 12.7 3.86 12.7 7C12.7 10.14 10.14 12.7 7 12.7C5.48908 12.6974 4.0408 12.096 2.97241 11.0276C1.90403 9.9592 1.30264 8.51092 1.3 7C1.3 3.86 3.86 1.3 7 1.3ZM7 0C3.14 0 0 3.14 0 7C0 10.86 3.14 14 7 14C10.86 14 14 10.86 14 7C14 3.14 10.86 0 7 0ZM8 3H6V8H8V3ZM8 9H6V11H8V9Z"></path></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:!text-current [&amp;_kbd]:!text-current [&amp;_a]:!text-current [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:!text-current text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">When a dispute is <strong>lost</strong>, Whop does not auto-create a Refund record and you cannot create one yourself. <code>payments.refund</code> rejects calls against a disputed payment with “This payment has been disputed. Therefore, it cannot be refunded.” Track the lost dispute as the source of truth and do not expect a <code>refund.created</code> event.</span></div></div>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="permissions"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#permissions" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="gray" height="12px" viewBox="0 0 576 512"><path d="M0 256C0 167.6 71.6 96 160 96h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C98.1 144 48 194.1 48 256s50.1 112 112 112h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C71.6 416 0 344.4 0 256zm576 0c0 88.4-71.6 160-160 160H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c61.9 0 112-50.1 112-112s-50.1-112-112-112H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c88.4 0 160 71.6 160 160zM184 232H392c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H184c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Permissions</span></h2>
<div data-table-wrapper="true" class="overflow-x-auto flex w-[calc(100%+(var(--page-padding)*2))] my-[1em] py-[1em] -mx-[var(--page-padding)] max-w-none [contain:inline-size]"><div class="px-[var(--page-padding)] grow max-w-none table"><table class="m-0 min-w-full w-full max-w-none table [&amp;_td]:min-w-[150px] [&amp;_th]:text-left [&amp;_td[data-numeric]]:tabular-nums"><thead><tr><th>Action</th><th>Required permission</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Create a refund</td><td><code>payment:manage</code></td></tr><tr><td>Retrieve / list refunds</td><td><code>payment:basic:read</code></td></tr><tr><td>Retrieve / list disputes</td><td><code>payment:dispute:read</code></td></tr><tr><td>Update or submit evidence</td><td><code>payment:dispute</code></td></tr></tbody></table></div></div>
<span data-as="p">Add these from the <a class="link" href="/developer/guides/permissions">Permissions guide</a>.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="next-steps"><div class="absolute" tabindex="-1"><a href="#next-steps" class="-ml-10 flex items-center opacity-0 border-0 group-hover:opacity-100 focus:opacity-100 focus:outline-0 group/link" aria-label="Navigate to header">​<div class="w-6 h-6 rounded-md flex items-center justify-center shadow-sm text-gray-400 dark:text-white/50 dark:bg-background-dark dark:brightness-[1.35] dark:ring-1 dark:hover:brightness-150 bg-white ring-1 ring-gray-400/30 dark:ring-gray-700/25 hover:ring-gray-400/60 dark:hover:ring-white/20 group-focus/link:border-2 group-focus/link:border-primary dark:group-focus/link:border-primary-light"><svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" fill="gray" height="12px" viewBox="0 0 576 512"><path d="M0 256C0 167.6 71.6 96 160 96h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C98.1 144 48 194.1 48 256s50.1 112 112 112h72c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H160C71.6 416 0 344.4 0 256zm576 0c0 88.4-71.6 160-160 160H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c61.9 0 112-50.1 112-112s-50.1-112-112-112H344c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24h72c88.4 0 160 71.6 160 160zM184 232H392c13.3 0 24 10.7 24 24s-10.7 24-24 24H184c-13.3 0-24-10.7-24-24s10.7-24 24-24z"></path></svg></div></a></div><span class="cursor-pointer">Next steps</span></h2>
